planning_end:\n                u = x.get('units')\n                if u is None and x.get('crates') is not None:\n                    u = float(x['crates']) * ratio\n                if u is None:\n is None or additional_units_needed <= 0:\n            crates_required = 0\n            rounding_applied = False\n        else:\n            raw = additional_units_needed / ratio\n            crates_required = int(math.ceil(raw))\n            rounding_applied = (raw != crates_required)\n\n        # Required delivery date logic\n        if crates_required == 0:\n            required_delivery = None\n        else:\n            if projected_stockout is None:\n                required_delivery = None\n            else:\n                if earliest_delivery is not None and earliest_delivery <= projected_stockout:\n                    if delivered_doh_to_end is None:\n                        required_delivery = projected_stockout\n                    else:\n                        required_delivery = as_of_date + datetime.timedelta(days=int(math.floor(delivered_doh_to_end)))\n                else:\n                    required_delivery = projected_stockout\n\n        if crates_required > 0:\n            earlier_required = (earliest_delivery is None) or (required_delivery is not None and required_delivery < earliest_delivery)\n        else:\n            earlier_required = False\n\n        results.append({\n            'Part_Code': part_code,\n            'Current_Units': int(current_units) if isinstance(current_units, int) else current_units_num,\n 'Additional_Units_Needed': additional_units_needed,\n            'Crates_Required_Rounded_Up': crates_required,\n            'Earliest_Scheduled_Delivery_Date': earliest_delivery,\n            'Required_Delivery_Date': required_delivery,\n            'Rounding_Applied': bool(rounding_applied),\n            'Earlier_Delivery_Required': bool(earlier_required),\n        })\n\n    # 7.
